### 2026-11-13 09:11:21  #市場

【精品茅臺零售均價領跌 國窖1573止跌反彈 古井貢古20、青花郎漲幅居先】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，11月13日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價七漲三跌兩平，上漲品種的數量明顯勝出，累計漲幅也顯著超越下跌端，整體價盤在經歷三連跌後反彈。上漲端，五糧液普五八代上漲3元至789元，三連陽逼近790元一線，過去六天其價格有五天上漲，觸底反彈走勢明確；五糧液1618上漲3元至820元，連續兩天上漲且漲幅擴大；國窖1573上漲7元至859元，完全收復昨日失地，自過去一個月來最低點強勁反彈；洋河夢之藍M6+上漲4元至589元，連續第二天大幅上揚；古井貢古20上漲6元至516元，昨日平盤蓄力後發力上攻；習酒君品上漲1元至613元，連續第二天小幅上漲，低位有序整理；青花郎上漲6元至659元，終結三連陰頹勢，昨日該酒品零售均價重跌10元之多。下跌端，飛天茅臺的零售均價小跌1元至1765元，連續第四天下跌；精品茅臺下跌5元至2415元，四連陰再創近30天新低，i茅臺開始全天動態補貨模式投放吸引其市價下行；茅臺1935下跌1元至684元，結束過去兩天平盤走勢。持平方面，青花汾20仍為378元，連續第二天維持在這一水平；水晶劍南春平盤於400元關口，近期其價格多次穿行這一整百點位。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價大幅反彈，國窖1573、古井貢古20、青花郎的漲幅強勁，推動整體價盤止跌回升。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10487元，較昨日大幅上漲23元，創下3天以來新高。

### 2026-11-13 09:06:07  #公司 #國際

【英偉達CEO的最後時光 知情人士透露：黃仁勳最後開始頻繁使用“隨便”和“我不在乎”等字眼】據報道，到最後時刻，黃仁勳看起來已經身心俱疲、徹底崩潰，他傳奇般的精力也消耗殆盡。在連續數週否認不忠指控並竭力保住英偉達公司執行長的職位後，他於週三深夜做出了無條件投降。據知情人士透露，黃仁勳以往那種大權在握、發號施令的作風已蕩然無存，他開始頻繁使用“隨便吧”和“我不在乎”這樣的字眼。在將英偉達打造成市值數萬億美元的AI晶片巨頭的數十年裡，黃仁勳以對細節的極致關注而聞名，小到晶片架構，大到公司最基層員工的薪酬方案，他都要過問。但如今，在在場人士的眼中，他已經徹底放棄。對於這位億萬富翁和科技巨頭而言，這場隕落來得極其迅速。就在兩週前，他還是全球人工智慧領域的標誌性人物。他憑藉直率和活力，不僅贏得了美國總統唐納德·特朗普的青睞，塑造了全球科技政策，還在他如同教皇出訪般的國際行程中吸引了無數狂熱的追隨者。

### 2026-11-13 09:02:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Stock Market #Importance 1

Japanese Shares Head for Weekly Gains — The Nikkei 225 Index climbed 1.5% to above 61,700 on Friday, posting gains in four of the past five sessions and putting the index up more than 7% for the week, its strongest weekly performance since June. Japanese equities recovered from Thursday’s losses as investors reassessed the broader implications of Nvidia’s leadership changes for the artificial intelligence sector. Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang stepped down following reports of extramarital affairs, with CFO Colette Kress assuming the role of interim CEO. Meanwhile, market participants are anticipating Anthropic’s highly awaited IPO next week, which has attracted orders equivalent to roughly six times the shares available, highlighting strong investor demand for the AI company. Technology and AI-related stocks led the market rebound, including Kioxia Holdings (2.8%), Advantest (6.4%), Tokyo Electron (2.9%), Lasertec (2.9%) and Ibiden Co (2.5%).

### 2026-11-13 08:54:29  #市場 #國際

【汽油價格高企 但美國人駕車出行熱情未減】根據彭博新能源財經的分析，自伊朗戰爭導致美國汽油價格飆升以來，美國人駕車出行幾乎沒有減少。經通脹調整後的汽油零售價格在5月達到峰值，比戰前的12月至2月平均水平高出53%，超過2008年油價飆升、2011年利比亞內戰以及2022年俄烏戰爭所致漲幅。然而，經季節性因素調整後，5月車輛行駛里程僅比戰前水平下降0.3%，並在6月回升。與以往衝擊相比，一個重要的區別在於消費者的汽油成本負擔。儘管汽油價格上漲，但3月至8月汽油支出僅佔可支配收入的約2.3%，約為2008年和2011年水平的一半。負擔減輕，加上勞動力市場更為強勁，或許可以解釋為何駕車出行依然保持穩定。

### 2026-11-13 08:53:56  #央行 #資料 #市場 #國際

【中信期貨：美元與利率形成雙重壓制，黃金配置需求尚難扭轉調整】黃金連續第三個交易日下跌。美國兩年期國債收益率本週累計上行約20個基點，美元指數在週二、週三累計上漲近1%，持有成本上升與美元走強共同壓制金價，前期反彈未能延續。 美國10月CPI與核心CPI環比分別上漲0.4%和0.3%，均高於預期，核心價格壓力持續成為政策收緊的依據。古爾斯比對通脹走勢表達明顯擔憂，疊加沃勒此前提出更大幅度加息的可能性，市場已充分計入12月加息25個基點，並開始交易加息50個基點的風險。油價回落雖有助於緩解能源通脹，但尚未消除核心通脹黏性，短期難以推動緊縮預期明顯降溫。 配置需求仍為黃金提供支撐。部分黃金ETF恢復增持，央行9月購金亦有所加快，表明價格回落後配置資金開始入場。但投機資金迴流缺乏持續性，官方購金和ETF買盤尚難完全抵消美元、利率上行帶來的壓力。黃金跌破4000美元/盎司，也說明配置需求對價格的支撐仍會受到宏觀環境變化的考驗。（中信期貨）

Australian Equities Slip Further but Heads for Third Weekly Gain — Australian stocks fell 73 points, or 0.8%, to 9,074 in Friday morning trade, extending prior losses amid broad-based declines, led by logistics, tech, and non-energy minerals. Caution prevailed ahead of next week's October labor data after unemployment stayed near a five-year high in September as employment eased. Higher borrowing costs in both the U.S. and Australia also dampened appetite for risk assets. A negative lead from Wall Street overnight added to the pressure after the fallout from Nvidia’s leadership upheaval. SGH Ltd. (-3.2%), Codan Ltd. (-2.5%), and Qantas Airways (-1.9%) were among major laggards. The big four banks lost 0.1–0.6%, while BHP Group slipped 0.7% on weaker iron ore prices and a stronger U.S. dollar. 【CITIC Securities: Tech leadership changes could amplify short-term market volatility but systemic sell-off risk likely limited】CITIC Securities said NVIDIA CEO Jensen Huang’s departure, combined with shifts in the US political backdrop, could trigger a valuation repricing across the AI supply chain and a style rebalance in US equities, though the medium‑term effect depends on corporate earnings evolution. In tech, markets may short‑term reprice slower AI capex growth and downward revisions to semiconductor earnings; supply‑chain inventory risk appears limited and hardware downside should be manageable. Slower AI capex would improve cloud service providers’ (CSPs’) free cash flow, and, together with a potential retreat in long‑end yields, could support the SaaS sector. For US equities, the midterm elections and tech leadership turnover may increase near‑term volatility but systemic selling pressure is likely limited; as political risk is digested, pricing should refocus on earnings growth and upward revisions to underpin medium‑term performance.

【中信證券：科技龍頭企業管理層變動可能加劇短期市場波動，但系統性拋售壓力或相對有限】中信證券研報稱，英偉達宣佈創始人兼執行長黃仁勳離任，疊加美國政治格局變化，或引發AI產業鏈估值重估及美股市場風格再平衡，但對美股中期走勢的影響仍取決於企業盈利基本面的演變。科技層面，市場短期可能對AI資本開支放緩及半導體企業盈利預期下修進行重新定價，但產業鏈庫存風險相對有限，硬體板塊調整幅度或較為可控。AI資本開支增速放緩有助於改善CSP的自由現金流狀況，疊加長端利率潛在回落，SaaS板塊有望受益。美股層面，美國中期選舉及科技龍頭企業管理層變動可能加劇短期市場波動，但系統性拋售壓力或相對有限。隨著政治風險逐步消化，美股定價邏輯或由短期風險情緒主導逐步迴歸企業盈利基本面，盈利增長及盈利預期上修有望繼續為市場中期表現提供支撐。

### 2026-11-13 08:23:37  #市場

【銀河證券：電子行業具備超跌反彈的基本面基礎 關注AI超跌修復視窗期】中國銀河證券研報釋出11月電子行業展望與配置思路。電子行業具備超跌反彈的基本面基礎，關注AI超跌修復視窗期。進入11月，市場重心將從題材預期轉向真實業績兌現，優先選擇三季報已經驗證盈利、四季度景氣延續、估值匹配的標的，關注三條主線：（1）儲存與先進封裝：算力拉動儲存緊俏，原廠長協鎖定遠期產能，先進封裝是算力晶片關鍵瓶頸。重點跟蹤儲存合約價格、大廠擴產、工藝良率。（2）AI-PCB/CCL/MLCC：三季報盈利兌現充分，業績和估值回撥形成反差，建議跟蹤上游原材料緊缺程度、產品交期、海外雲廠商資本開支等。（3）國產裝置、材料與算力晶片：政策持續加持，晶圓廠擴產帶動國產化落地，前期回撥消化估值，關注招標落地進度，留意外部出口管制不確定性。

US Stock Futures Climb as Nvidia Shares Stabilize — US stock futures moved higher on Friday as selling pressure on Nvidia shares eased following the resignation of CEO Jensen Huang amid an ongoing scandal. Futures tied to the Dow Jones, S&P 500, and Nasdaq 100 gained between 0.2% and 0.6%. In after-hours trading, Nvidia shares advanced more than 1%, rebounding from Thursday’s losses as investors evaluated the potential leadership transition at the chipmaker, with CFO Colette Kress assuming the role of interim CEO. Wall Street’s major indexes ended lower on Thursday, with the Dow falling 0.36%, while the S&P 500 and Nasdaq Composite declined 0.35% and 0.39%, respectively. Investors are now looking ahead to October’s PPI report for further indications of inflationary pressures. A stronger-than-expected consumer inflation reading, coupled with hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials, has reinforced expectations that interest rates could be raised more aggressively.

【華泰證券：油價回落或催化航空板塊反彈】華泰證券研報稱，由於前期油價衝擊且旺季順價較弱，第三季度航空板塊重倉持股佔比進一步下降，市場關注度與預期處於低位。不過基本面改善訊號陸續出現，油價下跌、票價改善、監管強化反內卷導向等因素驅動板塊反彈。同時第三季度航司盈利雖同比大幅下滑，但有望見底。華泰證券認為，若油價進一步回落及其預期強化，航司有望重回盈利釋放週期，看好帶動板塊趨勢性反轉。機場方面，第三季度旅客吞吐量同比增速回升，上市機場盈利整體較為平穩，不過變現能力仍未明顯改善，流量仍是機場盈利主要驅動因素，需關注產能週期及對成本的影響。

Venezuela Economic Growth Accelerates in Q3 — Venezuela’s economy expanded by 7.42% year-on-year in the third quarter of 2026, accelerating from a 7.14% growth in the previous period. This marked the 22nd consecutive quarter of economic expansion and the strongest in a year. Growth was primarily driven by non-oil activity, which increased 6.28%. Construction remained the strongest-performing sector, surging 35.75%, supported by investment to repair infrastructure damaged by the twin earthquakes at the end of the second quarter. Financial and insurance activities rose 29.29%, while agriculture expanded 7.13%, accommodation and food services (tourism) grew 8.95%, and manufacturing advanced 4.32%. The private sector as a whole recorded solid growth of 8.02%. Meanwhile, gross fixed capital formation increased 28.25%, pointing to stronger investment and confidence in the domestic productive sector.

【美國白宮將CFTC與SEC人選提交參議院審批】週四，白宮將針對美國商品期貨交易委員會（CFTC）和美國證券交易委員會（SEC）的兩黨提名名單正式提交給參議院，等待批准。此舉正值上週中期選舉失利後，總統唐納德·特朗普所在的共和黨即將於明年1月失去參議院控制權之際。民主黨人克里斯汀·約翰遜（Kristin Johnson）被提名為SEC委員，其任期將於2029年屆滿。她此前曾擔任CFTC委員，由時任民主黨總統喬·拜登任命。週四公佈的另一位SEC提名人是共和黨人史蒂文·萊文（Steven Levine），他將出任該機構委員，任期至2030年屆滿。民主黨人馬修·羅蘭（Matthew Rowland）被提名為CFTC委員，以填補剩餘任期至2028年屆滿的空缺。共和黨人內森·阿諾尼克（Nathan Anonick）同樣被提名為CFTC委員，其任期將於2031年屆滿。由特朗普挑選並於2025年獲參議院確認的CFTC主席邁克·塞利格（Mike Selig）在社交平臺X上發帖稱，羅蘭和阿諾尼克是“兩位強有力的跨黨派人選”，並表示期待與他們共事。

### 2026-11-13 07:36:44  #公司

【英偉達臨時CEO科萊特·克雷斯曾試圖管控公司最高風險的業務押注】據報道，英偉達於週四任命其資深首席財務官科萊特·克雷斯（Colette Kress）為臨時CEO，這位財務老將將被迫帶領公司度過因前任CEO黃仁勳突然辭職而引發的充滿不確定性的動盪風暴。現年59歲的克雷斯於2013年加入英偉達，當時該公司還只是一家默默無聞的遊戲晶片供應商。儘管她在引導公司崛起並在AI經濟中佔據核心地位的過程中發揮了重要作用，但據知情人士透露，她並不是公司重大決策的拍板人。黃仁勳對英偉達擁有絕對控制權，且從未制定過明確的繼任計劃。一位瞭解克雷斯的人士表示，每當兩人產生分歧時，黃仁勳往往會“強行壓制”她的意見。儘管如此，克雷斯在投資界依然廣為人知。她是除黃仁勳之外，唯一一位定期在英偉達季度財報電話會議上發言的高管。下一次財報電話會議定於下週二舉行。與黃仁勳相比，克雷斯可能會採取更為保守的策略。作為財務主管，她有時會對黃仁勳力推的交易提出反對意見。據知情人士透露，其中包括一項提議由英偉達為OpenAI提供高達250億美元信用擔保的交易，該交易旨在讓OpenAI利用英偉達最新一代晶片填滿8吉瓦的資料中心空間。今年8月，英偉達最終將該交易一分為二，目前僅承諾為第一部分提供約100億美元的信用擔保。據上述人士透露，近幾個月來，由於資料中心專案延期以及雲端計算交易取消，AI熱潮的圖景變得愈發複雜，克雷斯因此將工作重心放在尋找應對之策，以確保英偉達的財務風險不會過度集中在少數幾家公司身上。在加入英偉達之前，克雷斯曾擔任思科（Cisco）一個業務部門的首席財務官長達三年，並在微軟（Microsoft）工作了十餘年。

### 2026-11-13 07:19:57  #公司 #國際

【Anthropic與OpenAI被控抄襲神經網路專利】週四，一家由電腦科學家創立的人工智慧公司提起訴訟，指控Anthropic PBC和OpenAI OpCo涉嫌侵犯專利權。Flinn人工智慧技術有限責任公司（Flinn AI）向美國特拉華州地區法院提交了兩份訴狀，指控Anthropic和OpenAI均侵犯了由聯合發明人史蒂文·弗林（Steven Flinn）和娜奧米·曼尼佩尼（Naomi Moneypenny）獲得的五項專利。訴狀稱，這些發明可追溯至2015年末和2016年初，當時弗林與聯合發明人曼尼佩尼提交了專利申請，其“旨在保護這一代全新的基於注意力機制的神經網路技術，而該技術如今構成了現代生成式人工智慧系統的重要功能基礎”。針對Anthropic的訴訟主要聚焦於Claude系列大語言模型。訴狀稱，隨著該公司不斷推出更復雜的迭代版本，其侵權行為一直在持續。Flinn AI在訴狀中指出：“隨著Claude模型家族及其相關產品的每一次迭代與擴充套件，Anthropic的系統提供了更先進的基於注意力機制的推理、檢索、記憶、智慧體（Agentic）以及多模態功能，這些功能均侵犯了涉案專利。”針對OpenAI的訴狀中也包含了類似的段落，指控其隨著模型日益先進，侵權行為同樣在不斷升級。此外，弗林還創立了ManyWorlds Inc.公司，曼尼佩尼隨後也加入了該公司。今年早些時候，另一家名稱相似的公司Many Worlds 2T Innovations LLC曾起訴OpenAI和Spotify，指控它們侵犯了弗林與曼尼佩尼名下另一組與內容推薦相關的專利。

### 2026-11-13 07:11:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Peru #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Peru Raises Rate for First Time in Almost 4 Years — Peru’s Central Reserve Bank (BCRP) raised its benchmark interest rate by 25 bps to 4.50% in November 2026, marking its first hike in nearly four years, in line with expectations. The move reflected supply shocks, higher inflation expectations and strong domestic demand. Annual inflation accelerated to 5.3% in October from 4.5% in September, mainly due to higher food and energy prices. Inflation excluding food and energy rose to 4.6% from 4.5%, while the measure excluding food, energy and transportation stood at 2.0%, within the 1%-3% target range. Twelve-month inflation expectations remained at 3.1%, above the target ceiling. The BCRP expects inflation to return to target and converge toward 2% as supply shocks fade, but warned that El Niño and Middle East tensions could prolong price pressures. Activity indicators remained robust in October, while business expectations slowed but stayed optimistic. The bank said the hike does not necessarily mark a tightening cycle.

### 2026-11-13 07:00:30  #其他

【經濟日報金觀平：延伸產業鏈實現算力“價值輸出”】文章指出，我國發展算力產業擁有明顯優勢。一是規模優勢。二是能源優勢。三是效能優勢。也要看到，部分地區算力建設仍存在一些問題。比如，有的地方把算力產業簡單看作“建機房、上機架、買伺服器”，重建設輕運營、重硬體輕生態，算力建得快、用得少。把算力資源轉化為發展優勢，必須積極延伸產業鏈，實現算力的“價值輸出”。當前，我國區域發展不平衡問題依然突出，西部地區迫切需要找到能夠突破地理和自然條件約束、融入全球高階產業分工的發展新路徑。算力建設為打破傳統產業分工格局、縮小東西部發展差距提供了重要契機。

NZX 50 Retreats from Record High but Poised for 3rd Weekly Gain — The NZX 50 dropped 80 points, or 0.6%, to 14,179 in Friday morning trade, tracking a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight amid a plunge in Nvidia shares following the resignation of CEO Huang, while rising oil prices lifted Treasury yields. Traders also took profit after the broader index hit a record high a day earlier, supported by strong earnings reports from heavyweight stocks. Fresh data also pressured sentiment, as growth in New Zealand's manufacturing sector eased in October amid rising fuel prices, the cost of living, and concerns over election uncertainty. Rising oil prices further pressured the index, as inflation concerns and expectations of interest rate hikes increased. Almost all sectors traded in the red, led by industrials, with notable losers including Auckland International Airport (-3.4%), Turners Automotive (-1.9%), Fletcher Building (-1.6%), and Brisco Group (-1.2%). However, the index was up 1.3% for the week, marking its third straight weekly gain.

【一微科技登陸港交所 18C板塊擴容至29家】一微科技今日正式登陸港交所主機板，為港股18C板塊再添一家成員。一微科技在上市首日表現亮眼，收盤漲38.18%至13.68港元/股，總市值為59億港元。統計顯示，包括一微科技在內，今年根據第18C章規則上市的公司為21家，遠超2025年的5家和2024年的3家。目前18C板塊總共有29家公司，股價表現分化明顯。一微科技是一家領先的智慧移動機器人技術平臺提供商，以Amicro Core為核心構建模組化、軟硬體一體化架構的智慧移動機器人技術平臺，整合了機器人專用SoC、模組化元件、感測器（硬體層面），及演算法、SDK、工具鏈（軟體層面）等整合技術體系，為多元應用場景提供系統級的解決方案。根據弗若斯特沙利文的資料，以2025年收入計，一微科技在中國智慧移動機器人解決方案提供商中排名第三，市場份額約為4.2%；為中國最大的單模態機器人解決方案提供商，市場份額為2.2%；在中國多模態機器人解決方案提供商中排名第三，市場份額為5.9%。(證券時報)

### 2026-11-13 06:09:20  #公司

【騰訊謀變 馬化騰：AI大浪已打來，不能還坐著欣賞】11月11日，騰訊迎來成立28週年。按照慣例，這一天是騰訊的“Linktime”日，董事會主席馬化騰攜核心高管與全體員工線上對話，覆盤得失、校準方向。與往年不同的是，今年的這場直播馬化騰提示風險，要求員工適應AI變化。這次對話很快被傳成騰訊要裁員50%。11月12日，接近騰訊的知情人士表示，引發外界誤讀的，是管理層用來解釋資源排程的一組比喻。知情人士還原，在騰訊28週年的Linktime線上直播中，騰訊管理層主動表達了要加快組織變革的決心，同時特別提到，這和幾年前那次“降本增效”不一樣。前一次相對被動，更偏“降本”，這一次是面向AI時代的主動出擊，更偏“增效”，激發團隊去創造新的價值，鼓勵一線員工積極舉手向前衝，中間的管理層則需要變得更扁平和靈敏。為了應對新的變革，內部需要主動思考新打法和新戰場，50%的人像陸軍一樣，守土有責，透過提效去做好現有的核心業務；另50%的人像海軍一樣，擁抱浪潮，探索AI Native、拓展新的發展空間。知情人士表示，這是公司資源適應AI時代的重新排程，可能是所謂“裁員50%”的謠言誤讀出處，該說法完全不屬實。騰訊內部人士透露，公司內部業務眾多，有些業務已經在AI上做得比較好，有些動作不大，管理層的意思是希望沒有動員起來的要動起來，不要等靠。“這是內部講話，是一個概述，傳遞一個發展方向，並不是發內部檔案。”騰訊內部人士表示。(證券時報)

### 2026-11-13 05:44:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

NZ Manufacturing PMI in Expansion for 16th Straight Month — New Zealand’s BusinessNZ Performance of Manufacturing Index eased to 54.9 in October 2026 from 56.3 in September, marking a 16-month streak of expansion. Respondents cited fuel prices, the cost of living, and election uncertainty as concerns, though many pointed to the Christmas build-up and steady orders as positive factors. Employment was the weakest sub-index at 51.9, though still expanding, while Deliveries (61.4) and Finished Stocks (58.0) were the strongest. New Orders (54.3) and Production (53.9) eased from the previous month but remained comfortably in expansion. The Christmas build-up and steady infrastructure work suggest manufacturers are ending the year on a solid footing, even as input prices and the cost of living continue to weigh on the sector.

### 2026-11-13 05:34:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Import Prices YoY #Importance 1

South Korea Import Prices Rise in October — South Korea’s import prices rose 13.5% year on year in October 2026, following a 17.8% increase in September. Gains were led by raw materials, which climbed 36.2%, while intermediate goods prices rose 9.2%. In contrast, capital goods prices fell 4.2% and consumer goods prices declined 3.3%. By economic sector, mining product prices surged 40.7%, driven by a 47.6% jump in coal, crude petroleum and natural gas amid elevated international crude oil and gas prices caused by supply disruptions from the US-Iran war. Manufactured goods prices rose 4.8%, led by a 52.5% increase in coal and petroleum products. Meanwhile, prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products fell 1.9%, weighed down by an 8% drop in livestock products. The Net Barter Terms of Trade Index increased 21.3%, while the Import Price Index decreased 1.8% from the previous month.

South Korea Export Prices Rise in October — South Korea’s export prices surged 36.7% year on year in October 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 41.3% increase in September. Prices of manufactured goods rose 36.7%, led by a 95.9% jump in computers, electronic, and optical equipment. Coal and petroleum product prices also surged 77.2%, while prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products rose 25.4%. The Export Volume Index increased 14.9% and the Export Value Index rose 36.6%. Meanwhile, the Export Price Index edged up 0.7% from the previous month.

【NVIDIA interim CEO may pursue more cautious strategy than Jensen Huang】The Information reports interim CEO and CFO Colette Kress has repeatedly objected to high‑risk deals pushed by Jensen Huang and may pursue a more conservative operating strategy. Kress opposed a prior plan for NVIDIA to provide up to $250 bln in credit support to OpenAI; that package was split and NVIDIA now commits about $100 bln. After data center project delays and cancelled cloud deals, Kress has moved to reduce NVIDIA’s concentration risk—the company has already provided more than $100 bln in investments and credit guarantees to customers and supply‑chain partners. Some investors fear financing support could be tightened after Huang’s departure; one cloud customer is reportedly weighing a switch to Google or AMD chips. Kress must also navigate customer procurement diversification, tighter U.S. export controls and internal power realignments; she has previously clashed with Huang and his family on company matters.

【DALY: Further hikes may be needed to curb inf】San Francisco Fed President DALY said further Fed tightening may be necessary to reduce inf, noting persistent price pressures that are not confined to industries directly affected by tariffs or oil. She said an increasing number of firms report plans to pass higher input costs to consumers and that pricing willingness is strengthening. DALY said she supports a September rate hike and holding policy unchanged in October. On AI, she said AI infrastructure is raising prices in some areas but its overall effect on inf is so far modest and not broad-based; she expects AI to boost productivity over the long run but the timing is uncertain. DALY is not an FOMC voter this year.

【US October fiscal deficit $404bn, up 42% YoY】The US Treasury said the first month of fiscal 2027 (Oct 2026) posted a $404bn deficit, up 42% YoY. Outlays rose 12% YoY to $773bn, driven by higher military, healthcare and net interest spending. Receipts fell 9% to $369bn; tariff revenue dropped about 39% to $19.2bn and larger tariff and corporate tax refunds also weighed on receipts. Treasury projects the fiscal-year deficit will widen modestly to roughly $2.2trn, versus $1.99trn a year earlier (about 6% of GDP).

### 2026-11-13 04:33:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Extends Losses on Fiscal Concerns — The Brazilian real weakened to over 5.65 per USD in November, extending its lowest since since May of last year, as the government’s fiscal adjustment bill disappointed markets. Markets had previously welcomed the news as an initial step toward a more robust fiscal agenda, but now view the bill as a timid adjustment. The measures are understood not to focus on structural adjustments to reverse the growing public debt trajectory, but rather on opening more room for spending freezes to reach the 0.5% of GDP surplus target for 2027. The government has faced market pressure to announce fiscal adjustments after Brazilian assets plunged following President Lula’s reelection. Meanwhile, annual inflation rose to 4.63% in October from 4.58% in September, below market expectations of 4.74% but marking a four-month high and remaining above the BCB’s 4.5% target ceiling. With inflation still above target, the central bank is expected to remain cautious about resuming its Selic easing cycle.

Brent Crude Rises on Uncertainty Over US-Iran Diplomacy — Brent crude rose to around $96 a barrel on Thursday, extending gains for a second consecutive session and rebounding from a ten-week low of $91.5 reached on November 10th. Prices climbed after US President Trump declared that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran. The remarks marked a sharp departure from Monday, when Trump described the war as "militarily effectively over" following talks with Qatar's prime minister. Meanwhile, Iran has shown little willingness to soften its negotiating position. Mohsen Rezaee, secretary of Iran's Supreme National Security Council, said Tehran would not remove any enriched nuclear material from the country. Despite the diplomatic uncertainty, Middle Eastern crude supplies have recovered considerably. Saudi Arabia reported a 26% increase in October production, while Iraq also raised output. However, shipments remain dependent on alternative routes, ship-to-ship transfers and military protection.

US Stocks Fall on Nvidia Crisis — US stock indices moved lower on Thursday as Nvidia’s leadership crisis curbed risk appetite and an oil price rally lifted Treasury yields. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq each edged about 0.3% lower, while the Dow shed over 50 points. Nvidia tumbled 6% following the ouster of CEO Huang. The US is investigating Alice Huang, whom officials suspect may be a spy, over whether entities linked to her were involved in efforts to smuggle products to China. The chip sector was mixed, Micron fell nearly 2.5% and Intel lost over 1%, while Broadcom and AMD gained over 5% each. Rising oil prices also weighed on sentiment, reviving energy inflation concerns. Bond yields climbed on expectations that the Federal Reserve will step up the pace of rate hikes to cool inflation, leaving credit-sensitive stocks mostly lower. Meanwhile, Alphabet rose over 2% on its investment in TPU production.

【克利夫蘭聯儲行長稱通脹正朝錯誤方向發展 利率需更具限制性】克利夫蘭聯儲行長貝絲·哈馬克重申，她認為在人工智慧投資所帶來的需求壓力下，需要提高利率以抗擊通脹。“我的預期是我們需要將政策轉向限制性立場，以幫助通脹回落至2%的目標，這一點至關重要，”哈馬克週四在俄亥俄州哥倫布的活動上表示。“如果我們不這樣做，我擔心通脹思維可能開始紮根。”哈馬克表示，面對資料中心建設帶來的需求壓力，美聯儲有充分的應對能力。“當我權衡這些因素時，我看到的是通脹一直很頑固，而且持續時間比我預期的要長，”她說。剔除食品和能源價格的基礎通脹10月升幅超過預期。哈馬克表示，這份報告顯示，通脹方面的進展“確實停滯”。“我看到的最新資料顯示通脹正朝錯誤的方向發展。我們正在遠離2%的目標，而不是向這個目標靠攏，”她說。上月美聯儲決定將利率維持在3.75%-4%的區間不變，哈馬克是兩名持異議的官員之一。當時她傾向於加息25個基點。

### 2026-11-13 03:28:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Argentina Monthly Inflation Rate Hits Six-Month High — Argentina's monthly inflation rate rose to 2.2% in October 2026 from 1.9% in the prior month, marking a six-month high. Utilities recorded the largest price increase (4.2%), driven by higher gas prices. Recreation and culture followed, up 3.3%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages contributed the most to the monthly variation across most regions, rising 2% nationally. Prices also rose considerably for clothing and footwear (2.2%), healthcare (1.7%), transport (2.1%), communication (2%), and restaurants and hotels (1.7%). Seasonal prices rose 5.5% and regulated prices increased 2.9%. Core inflation eased to 1.5% from 1.7% in September. Year on year, Argentina's inflation eased slightly to 33.1% from 33.4%, missing estimates of 32.7%.

US Budget Deficit Widens More Than Forecast in October — The US government budget deficit widened to $404 billion in October 2026 from $284 billion a year earlier, missing forecasts of a $298.6 billion gap. Outlays rose to $773 billion from $689 billion. The largest expenditures were Medicare ($170 billion), Social Security ($143 billion), national defense ($103 billion), and net interest ($103 billion), followed by health ($94 billion), income security ($58 billion), and veterans' benefits ($54 billion). Meanwhile, receipts declined to $369 billion from $404 billion, with individual income taxes ($186 billion), social insurance and retirement ($131 billion), and customs duties ($19 billion) as the largest revenue sources.

【智利上調鋰產量預期，市場明年料重返過剩】智利上調了今年的鋰產量預期，儘管需求增長放緩和供應上升預計將推動全球市場在2027年重返過剩狀態。智利國家銅業委員會（Cochilco）週四表示，全球第三大鋰生產國智利在2026年將生產約32.5萬噸碳酸鋰當量（LCE），高於一年前預測的30.5萬噸。2027年產量預計將升至33.5萬噸。受電池儲能系統快速增長的推動，全球鋰需求今年預計將增長25%，達到208萬噸，較Cochilco一年前的預測高出約34%。但需求增長預計在2027年將大幅放緩至8%，屆時消費量將達到225萬噸。Cochilco預計今年全球將出現7.9萬噸的供應短缺，而一年前預測的是6萬噸的供應過剩。到2027年，隨著新供應逐步入市，供應增速預計將超過需求，推動市場重返4.2萬噸的過剩狀態。

### 2026-11-13 02:56:47  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【Chicago Fed's Goolsbee: US inf at 'dangerous moment'; data-center demand a watchpoint】Chicago Fed President Goolsbee said US inflation is at a "dangerous moment" after October CPI surprised to the upside, marking a second consecutive monthly increase. He had expected inflation to ease as tariff effects faded and oil fell, but that has not yet occurred. Goolsbee flagged persistent price pressure in services and healthcare that he says is unrelated to tariffs or oil. The Fed is trying to separate supply-side shocks (tariffs, prolonged high oil) from demand-side overheating tied to large-scale data-center construction. AI-related inflation remains concentrated in AI, tech and related sectors and has not yet broadened; the Fed is monitoring for spread. He warned that if data-center driven demand overheating starts to affect the wider economy it would become a problem. Proposals by newly elected Democratic governors to pause data-center builds are not directly connected to the Fed's inflation work.

TSX Falls on Miners and Banks Losses — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell about 0.5% to hover below 36,500 on Thursday amid losses in the banking and mining sectors. Miners posted losses as gold prices fell sharply, pressured by a stronger US dollar, expectations of further Fed tightening, and rising oil prices. Agnico Eagle lost nearly 3%, while Barrick and WPM shed over 1% each. Franco-Nevada edged lower after signaling its annual earnings would land at the midpoint of earlier guidance rather than the top half. Meanwhile, banks declined as rebounding oil prices amid escalating US-Iran tensions revived inflation fears and reinforced expectations of a restrictive monetary policy stance from both the BoC and the Fed. Scotiabank, TD Bank, and BMO lost near 0.5% each. In technology, Shopify fell over 0.5% amid the Nvidia crisis, which increased uncertainty in the sector. Celestica gained nearly 5.5%, extending an 8.4% surge from the prior session driven by recent investor-day commentary and strong third-quarter results.

### 2026-11-13 01:46:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Gasoline Futures Rise — US gasoline futures edged up to $2.64 per gallon but remained close to an eight-month low, as traders weighed Middle East tensions against recovering inventories. EIA data showed gasoline stocks rose by 1.8 million barrels in the week ending November 6, lifting domestic supplies to 1% above seasonal norms for the first time since April. Seasonal declines in driving demand ahead of winter and near-capacity refinery operations have supported the recovery. Still, uncertainty over a prolonged US-Iran conflict remains an upside risk. President Trump has downplayed the need for a deal with Iran, further dampening hopes of an imminent resolution, although crude continues to flow through the waterway. A shift by US refiners away from gasoline towards higher-margin middle distillates, alongside seasonal refinery maintenance, could further constrain supply. Escalating Ukrainian attacks on Russian refineries and lower Chinese exports are also contributing to tighter global fuel markets.

### 2026-11-13 01:40:13  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Lumber Slides Further as Housing Weakness Persists — Lumber futures fell below $490 per thousand board feet, reaching their lowest level since July 2024, amid persistent weakness in the US housing market. Existing-home sales fell to their lowest level in 16 years, as mortgage rates above 7% and stubbornly high inflation continued to deter buyers. Although mortgage rates declined for a fourth consecutive week to 7.11%, affordability remains strained. Construction activity is also weak, with spending on lumber-intensive single-family homes falling 2.4% year-on-year in September, while builder sentiment dropped to its lowest level since 2012 amid high input costs. Mill closures across North America could tighten supply as prolonged US-Canada tariff uncertainty remains a headwind. A Democratic sweep of Congress has raised prospects for repealing duties on Canadian lumber, although the Trump administration has yet to secure a trade agreement amid ongoing negotiations.

European Stocks Inch Higher — European stocks closed mixed on Thursday amid a batch of major corporate earnings results, while the ousting of Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang halted the recent rally for AI companies. The Euro STOXX 50 rose 0.1% to 6,399 and the STOXX Europe 600 extended its record high by 0.1% to 664. Siemens added 1.7% after it raised its medium-term targets on record order levels, largely due to higher demand for data center products and industrial factory automation. The insurance sector was also in the spotlight, with Allianz adding 2% after posting strong profits and raising its 2026 outlook, while Munich Re raised its profit guidance and jumped 2.9%. On the other hand, Enel lost 1.5% after its results. ASML fell 2% to pare its surge from earlier in the week, tracking the North American AI infrastructure sector following the leadership shake-up at Nvidia.

【英偉達新任董事會主席瓊斯給全體英偉達員工的信】黃仁勳離職後，英偉達新任董事會主席哈維·瓊斯於週四向員工傳送電子郵件，以下是信件的全文內容：
親愛的NVIDIA員工：

我寫這封信是為了分享一項重要人事變動。黃仁勳已辭去執行長及董事會成員職務，董事會已要求首席財務官科萊特·克雷斯出任臨時執行長，同時啟動永久繼任者的遴選工作，範圍涵蓋內部和外部候選人。我將出任公司首位董事會主席。

科萊特擔任首席財務官超過13年，是NVIDIA增長與資本戰略的關鍵設計者之一。她對公司業務及我們所處的市場有著深刻理解。董事會完全相信，科萊特能夠兌現我們對股東、客戶、合作伙伴以及每一位員工的承諾。

黃仁勳對NVIDIA的貢獻意義深遠。NVIDIA是全球最具影響力的科技公司之一。我們開創了加速計算，定義了現代GPU，並打造了驅動人工智慧的平臺。

董事會認真對待自身作為這家偉大公司守護者的職責。

公司的實力源於我們的員工、領導團隊、技術實力和合作伙伴生態。我相信，在科萊特的領導下，在董事會的全力支援下，我們將繼續推動業務增長，引領行業向前發展。感謝各位的專注、專業精神以及對NVIDIA的投入。

哈維·瓊斯
NVIDIA董事會主席

Brent Crude Rises from 10-Week Low — Brent crude rose above $94 a barrel on Thursday, extending gains for a second consecutive session as prices attempted to recover from a ten-week low of $91.5 on November 10. Investors weighed escalating tensions between Washington and Tehran against signs of recovering supply from Saudi Arabia. President Donald Trump said the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, signalling a shift away from recent diplomatic efforts. Tehran also maintained a hardline position, with senior official Mohsen Rezaee ruling out the removal of any enriched nuclear material from the country. Still, Saudi Arabia reported a 26% increase in oil production in October, bringing output to one of its highest monthly levels in years, while Iraq also raised production to a wartime record. Adding to supply-side pressure, EIA data showed US crude inventories rose by 4.657 million barrels in the week ended November 6, the largest increase since August and a reversal of five consecutive weekly declines.

US Crude Inventories Rise the Most in 3 Months — US crude oil inventories increased by 4.657 million barrels in the week ended November 6, marking the largest build since August and reversing five consecutive weeks of declines. Markets anticipated a 1.1 million-barrel draw. Stocks at the Cushing, Oklahoma, delivery hub rose by 106,000 barrels, while refinery crude processing increased by 322,000 barrels per day. Refined product inventories also recorded gains, with distillate stocks, including diesel and heating oil, climbing by 4.071 million barrels, the largest increase since July. Gasoline inventories rose by a further 1.799 million barrels. Meanwhile, net US crude imports increased by 544,000 barrels per day.

### 2026-11-13 01:03:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Cross $5,200 — Cocoa futures rose above $5,200 a tonne, pulling away from four-month lows hit on November 10, as supply concerns in top grower Ivory Coast intensified. The country's national weather agency (Sodexam) warned that cocoa production could fall between 21% and 25% in the 2026/27 season due to an exceptionally strong El Niño event. Annual rainfall is expected to decline by 10%–27%, shortening the rainy season and extending dry periods. Near-term supply flows are also facing disruption, as heavy rains have reportedly damaged roads in Ivory Coast, hampering bean deliveries to collection centres. Port arrivals remain well below year-ago levels, although dealers suggested that increased smuggling into neighbouring countries may partly explain the decline. However, subdued chocolate demand has contributed to a build-up in exchange inventories. ICE-monitored cocoa stocks have been rising since the start of the year, recently reaching a fresh 2.5-year high of 3,751,670 bags.

US Stock Indices Pare Losses — US stock indices pared losses on Thursday as markets assessed the AI infrastructure landscape after Jensen Huang resigned as CEO of Nvidia. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 inched above the flatline, while the Dow added over 100 points. Nvidia was down more than 5% after losing up to 14% earlier in the session as CFO Colette Kress was named interim CEO following Huang's resignation over his involvement with a suspected Chinese spy. Huang's stewardship saw Nvidia's participation in and orchestration of massive circular investments across all tiers of the AI sector, implicating global financial markets in the leadership change. Still, competitors AMD and Broadcom over 5%, while Alphabet's investment in TPU production drove its shares up near 3%. Meanwhile, Nebius sank nearly 16% after reporting delays in some data center capacity buildouts. Other sectors rebounded from Tuesday's selloff, which had been triggered by a hot CPI reading that bolstered bets on more Fed rate hikes next year.

### 2026-11-13 00:31:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Surges — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose above 5.05% on Thursday, extending its recent increase to a three-week high on expectations that the Federal Reserve will deliver multiple rate hikes in the current tightening cycle. CPI data released earlier in the week showed that both headline and core consumer prices rose more than expected in October, indicating that the surge in energy prices from the war in Iran was being transmitted to other sectors of the economy. Markets consequently piled into rate futures pricing in a Fed rate hike in December and two similar moves next year. Meanwhile, Treasury yields in the belly of the curve surged after Jensen Huang resigned as president and CEO of Nvidia, sparking fears of turmoil in the debt-laden AI sector. Because Nvidia has anchored and backstopped a massive wave of AI-related corporate bond issuance, a sudden freeze in the sector's credit activity risked triggering broader fixed-income selloffs as investors raised cash.

### 2026-11-13 00:01:23  #央行 #國際

【印尼人民幣清算行正式開業】印度尼西亞人民幣清算行12日在印尼首都雅加達正式開業，中國銀行擔任印尼人民幣清算行。來自中印尼兩國政府部門及企業、金融機構的約100名代表參加當日在印尼央行舉行的開業儀式。 目前，包括中國銀行、中國工商銀行、中國建設銀行、曼底利銀行、印尼國家銀行等在內的12家本地和外國銀行作為特許交叉貨幣做市商，成為人民幣清算行的參加行，相關交易基礎設施均已準備就緒。 印尼央行行長德斯特里·達瑪揚蒂在致辭中表示，隨著人民幣清算行的啟用及本幣交易機制的完善，企業可直接透過人民幣進行對華貿易結算，有助於降低對第三方貨幣的依賴，維護匯率穩定並增強區域經濟應對外部衝擊的韌性。（新華社）

European Natural Gas Soars by 7% — European natural gas prices surged 7% to €92.70/MWh, driven by forecasts of colder weather, supply disruptions in Norway and persistent geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Temperatures are expected to fall across Europe from November 17, with further cold spells possible in late November and early December, boosting heating demand. Meanwhile, Norwegian gas flows declined as outages intensified, particularly at the Troll field, where operator Gassco said the duration of the disruption remained uncertain. Supply concerns have been compounded by low storage levels: EU reserves stood below 72%, compared with a five-year seasonal average of 89%, while Germany’s stocks were below 60%. Geopolitical risks added further support after US President Donald Trump dismissed the need for a deal with Iran, while Tehran maintained a hardline stance on its enriched nuclear material.

【澤連斯基稱美俄談俄歐安全架構“有點奇怪”】烏克蘭總統澤連斯基12日在愛爾蘭首都都柏林舉行的歐洲政治共同體領導人會議上表示，烏方認為美國與俄羅斯討論建立俄歐新安全秩序的做法“有點奇怪”。他還說，烏克蘭正在尋求瞭解美國對“星盾”衛星通訊系統的限制細節，這些限制嚴重擾亂烏防空系統。澤連斯基說，根據烏方代表團反饋，日前在阿聯酋首都阿布扎比舉行的烏俄美三方會談期間，各方就安全保障問題進行了討論。他說，當美俄討論安全保障問題時，涉及的不僅是對烏克蘭的安全保障，實際上更多涉及歐洲與俄羅斯之間某種新的安全框架，“我們覺得這種做法有點奇怪”。烏方正等待了解更多細節，歐洲方面對此也應密切關注。澤連斯基表示，歐洲目前尚未直接參與有俄方代表在場的談判，但已介入相關程序，“我們需要確保歐洲能夠成為這些談判的固定參與方”。澤連斯基呼籲歐洲繼續向烏克蘭提供防空和能源支援，並出臺新的對俄製裁措施。澤連斯基還說，烏軍使用的“星盾”衛星通訊系統在烏克蘭暫時被佔領土再遭限速，影響到烏軍攔截俄羅斯噴氣式無人機的能力。烏方正向美方瞭解詳情，烏克蘭“絕不會以任何方式承認俄羅斯對烏領土的佔領”。（新華社）

Costa Rica GDP Growth Slows in Q3 2026 — Costa Rica’s economy expanded 2.7% year on year in Q3 2026, slowing from an upwardly revised 3.53% in the previous quarter and marking the weakest expansion since Q1 2021. Manufacturing output declined 3.7% after contracting 0.98% in Q2, and growth slowed across multiple sectors. Agriculture and fishing eased to 0.06% from 0.63%, while mining and quarrying edged down to 2.99% from 3.11%. Transport and warehousing fell to 2.06% from 3.11%, and accommodation and food services slowed to 2.83% from 3.6%. Financial activities and insurance eased to 4.71% from 5.82%, while growth in professional, scientific, technical, administrative and support services decelerated to 3.32% from 4.15%.

【華為新型儲存系統啟動試商用】11月12日，華為新型儲存系統試商用啟動現場會在貴安雲上屯舉行。會上，華為磁電儲存系統客戶聯合試商用正式啟動，標誌著該技術從實驗室驗證進入真實場景驗證階段。長期以來，企業級溫冷儲存的主流商用技術路線僅有HDD與磁帶兩條，核心技術與產能主要集中在少數海外廠商手中。磁電儲存系統是中國廠商首次定義並試商用的儲存新介質，可實現溫冷資料“存得起，取得快”，為溫冷儲存提供了第三條選擇路徑，有望成為AI時代溫冷資料新的儲存底座。目前華為磁電儲存系統已在華為雲烏蘭察布資料中心開始部署。據透露，該系統明年規劃的試商用容量已被客戶全量預定，供不應求。(上海證券報)

### 2026-11-12 23:06:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Existing Home Sales #Importance 3

US Existing Home Sales Lowest Since 2010 — Existing home sales in the US fell 5.5% to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 3.75 million units in October 2026, the lowest level since August 2010 and below market expectations of 3.85 million. NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun attributed the weakness to elevated mortgage rates, which have reached their highest levels since 2000, excluding one month in 2023. He noted that homeowners remain financially secure due to accumulated housing wealth, while lower mortgage rates could unlock significant pent-up demand. Sales were unchanged in the Northeast at 470,000 units, while the Midwest declined 3.2% to 900,000, the South fell 6.5% to 1.73 million and the West dropped 9.7% to 650,000. Meanwhile, housing inventory rose 1.3% from September and 4.6% year over year to 1.59 million homes, equivalent to 5.1 months of supply, up from 4.7 months in September. The median home price increased 0.4% from a year earlier to $416,700.

Colombia Consumer Confidence Hits Over One-Year Low — Colombia's Consumer Confidence Index fell 7.2 percentage points to 8.5% in October 2026, down from the prior month and marking its lowest level since September 2025. The Consumer Expectations Index declined 11.3 percentage points to 13.8%. Expectations for the general economic outlook deteriorated to 7.4% from 21.3%, while expectations for the country’s future economic conditions fell to 7.3% from 19.9%. Household economic outlook expectations weakened to 26.8% from 34.1%. Meanwhile, the Economic Conditions Index decreased 1.3 percentage points to 0.4%. Assessments of current household economic conditions deteriorated to -5.9% from -4.8%, and willingness to purchase durable goods fell to 6.7% from 8.1%.

Baltic Dry Index Advances for 7th Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, was up for a seventh straight session on Thursday, rising by 2.1% to its highest since October 5 at 3,020 points. Gains were broad-based across vessel segments, particularly the larger one. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, advanced by 2.9% to its highest since October 2 at 4,836 points. At the same time, the supramax index increased by 1.2% to an over one-week high of 1,831 points; and the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, went up 0.7% to its highest level since October 22 at 2,117 points.

### 2026-11-12 22:59:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Drops on Strong Dollar and Fed Rate Expectations — Silver prices fell around 3% to approximately $50 an ounce on Thursday, retreating to levels not seen in months as a stronger US dollar, rising oil prices and growing expectations of further Federal Reserve tightening weighed on the metal. Stronger-than-expected US consumer inflation data for October, released on Tuesday, pushed the dollar to a 19-month high and reinforced bets on further Fed rate hikes. Meanwhile, renewed gains in oil prices added to inflationary pressures after President Donald Trump said the US did not need a deal with Iran and redirected Vice President JD Vance towards domestic priorities rather than negotiations with Tehran. Romania Holds Interest Rate in November — The National Bank of Romania kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 6.50% on November 12, as expected, maintaining the Lombard lending rate at 7.50% and the deposit facility rate at 5.50%. The decision comes amid persistent inflationary pressures, with annual inflation rising to 6.94% in October from 6.17% in August, driven largely by higher fuel and energy prices. The central bank expects inflation to increase slightly further towards the end of 2026 before gradually declining, returning to its target range in the first quarter of 2028. Meanwhile, economic activity is expected to recover moderately in the second half of 2026, although fiscal consolidation, weaker consumption and the Middle East conflict pose risks to growth. The NBR also highlighted uncertainty surrounding energy and oil prices, exchange-rate developments and global financial conditions. The bank reaffirmed that maintaining price stability remains its priority amid elevated economic uncertainty.

### 2026-11-12 20:14:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Brazil Inflation Rate Rises Less Than Expected — Brazil’s annual inflation rose to 4.63% in October from 4.58% in September, below market expectations of an increase to 4.74%, marking a second consecutive monthly increase and the highest rate in four months. The acceleration was driven by higher price growth in food and beverages, which increased to 5.73% from 4.66% in September. Inflation also accelerated for household articles (2.88% vs. 2.08%) and communication (3.68% vs. 2.79%), while inflation for education edged up slightly to 5.96% from 5.95%. In contrast, inflation slowed for transport to 3.2% from 3.94% as fuel inflation softened to 4.3% from 4.97%. Price growth also slowed for housing (4.01% vs 4.22%), clothing (3.21% vs 3.34%), health and personal care (5.42% vs 5.75%) and personal expenses (5.8% vs 5.9%). From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.14% following a 0.82% rise in September, also below market expectations. As of October, inflation reached 4.1%.

### 2026-11-12 19:56:13  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bearish

【Multiple public REITs issue secondary-market volatility warnings】Secondary-market volatility in public REITs has risen. As of Nov 12, the CSI REITs total‑return index is down 7.36% YTD and the CSI REITs price index is down 11.73% YTD. On a monthly basis only January posted a gain (+3.98%); REITs have recorded nine consecutive monthly declines. Seventeen public REITs are positive YTD — China Merchants Expressway REIT, CICC Shandong Expressway REIT and ChinaAMC China Communications Construction REIT are up >10% — while 61 are negative YTD; 21 are down >20%, 10 down >30% and 3 down >40%, with industrial‑park and warehousing/logistics names most affected. Huatai Securities fixed‑income research says October’s core tension was improved expectations for incremental funding versus limited actual buying, producing a pattern of steady‑then‑weak, slow rises and fast falls: month‑start demand expansion plus falling rates supported allocations to cash‑flow‑stable, high‑distribution assets (eg guaranteed‑rental housing), while month‑end renewed trading supply, a rate uptick and five concentrated new REIT listings amplified price moves. Huatai adds deeply discounted industrial‑park and logistics REITs may present trading opportunities, but only after unlocked shares fully turnover, volumes ease and selling pressure is absorbed.

### 2026-11-12 19:56:08  #公司 #市場 #觀點

【多隻公募REITs釋出二級市場波動風險提示】近期，公募REITs二級市場波動加劇，截至11月12日，中證REITs全收益指數年內下跌7.36%，中證REITs指數同期下跌11.73%。單月來看，除今年1月上漲3.98%外，月線已實現9連跌。具體來看，目前有17只公募REITs年內錄得正漲幅，招商高速公路REIT、中金山東高速REIT、華夏中國交建REIT年內漲幅超10%；61只公募REITs年內收跌，21只跌幅超過20%，10只跌幅超過30%，更有3只跌幅超過40%，產業園、倉儲物流類成為“重災區”。華泰證券固收研究表示，10月公募REITs的核心矛盾是增量資金預期改善，但實際承接力量仍然有限，市場呈現前穩後弱、慢漲快跌的特徵。“月初需求擴容預期疊加利率下行，配置型資金增配現金流穩定、分派率保護較強的資產，如保租房等；月末市場重新交易供給壓力，利率上行和5只新REITs集中上市，少量減持便容易放大價格波動。”不過，華泰證券固收研究認為，產業園和倉儲物流中的深度折價品種可能具備交易機會，但需等待解禁份額充分換手、成交量回落和拋壓消化。（中國基金報）

### 2026-11-12 19:44:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tanzania #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Tanzania Inflation Rate Eases to 4.2% in October — The annual inflation rate in Tanzania edged down to 4.2% in October 2026 from 4.3% in each of the previous two months. Price growth cooled down mostly for food & non-alcoholic beverages (3.6% vs 3.9% in September); clothing & footwear (1.2% vs 1.7%); housing & utilities (2.3% vs 2.5%) and furnishings & household equipment (1.8% vs 2.6%). On the other hand, prices rose faster for transportation (14.9% vs 14%) and miscellaneous goods & services (3.5% vs 3.3%). On a monthly basis, the CPI fell by 0.3%, after increasing by 0.1% in the month earlier.

### 2026-11-12 19:39:13  #宏觀

【李強主持國務院第二十二次專題學習】11月12日，國務院以“健全多層次社會保障體系，進一步織密扎牢社會保障安全網”為主題，進行第二十二次專題學習。國務院總理李強在主持學習時強調，要深入學習貫徹習近平總書記關於社會保障工作的重要指示精神和黨中央決策部署，進一步深化社會保障制度改革，健全覆蓋全民、統籌城鄉、公平統一、安全規範、可持續的多層次社會保障體系，推動現代化建設成果更多更公平惠及全體人民。

中國人民大學教授鄭功成作講解。國務院副總理何立峰、劉國中，國務委員諶貽琴作交流發言。

李強在聽取講解和交流發言後指出，我國已基本建成世界上規模最大、功能完備的社會保障體系。當前經濟社會發展形勢呈現許多新特點，人口結構變化、技術進步、就業方式多樣化等對社會保障體系建設提出了新的更高要求。要充分認識社會保障在發展全域性中的重要作用，適應新形勢、解決新問題，推動社會保障事業高質量可持續發展。

李強指出，要堅持立足國情實際，發展中國特色社會保障體系。把握好盡力而為和量力而行的關係，把社會保障作為惠民生促發展的重要著力點，隨著經濟發展穩步提高社會保障水平。把握好政府主導和社會協同的關係，合理劃分單位、個人、政府、社會的責任，形成責任共擔、互助共濟的長效機制。把握好保障基本和多元供給的關係，在保基本、兜底線基礎上，努力豐富多元供給，更好滿足人民群眾多層次多樣化需求。

李強強調，要突出重點、加強協同，持續最佳化完善社會保障制度。加快補齊社會保障體系短板，擴大企業年金覆蓋面，提高個人養老金制度吸引力，支援商業健康保險發展壯大，穩妥有序推進長期護理保險制度建設。完善靈活就業人員社會保障制度，逐步提高參保率，加強新就業形態人員權益保障。推動社會保障制度更加公平，穩步提高城鄉居民養老保障水平，發揮好失業保險功能，健全分層分類的社會救助制度，完善社會福利制度。增強社保基金長期可持續性，完善並落實職工養老保險全國統籌制度，推進基本醫療保險省級統籌，強化社保基金安全監管和保值增值。要適應形勢變化，及時修訂完善社會保障相關法律法規，進一步夯實法治支撐。（新華社）

Israel’s Trade Deficit Widens Sharply — Israel’s trade deficit widened significantly to $5.18 billion in October 2026, up from $2.86 billion a year earlier. Imports surged 42.7% to $10.59 billion, their second-highest level on record, driven by increased purchases of raw materials, investment goods, consumer goods and fuels. Exports rose at a slower pace of 18.7% to $5.41 billion, supported by gains in manufacturing and mining. For the first ten months of 2026, the trade deficit reached $43.45 billion, compared with $32.04 billion over the same period in 2025.

### 2026-11-12 19:26:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Serbia Hikes Rate for 1st Time in 3 Years — The National Bank of Serbia hiked its key policy rate by 25bps to 6.0% in its November 2026 decision, breaking the streak of unchanged rates that started in October 2024. The Executive Board noted that prolonged uncertainty in the international environment due to the war in the Middle East continued to increase prices of oil, natural gas, and electricity, which recently triggered a jump in domestic refining margins. The Board cited increased risks of secondary effects on inflation from high energy prices as a reason for raising interest rates. Inflation rose to a 14-month high of 4.4% according to the latest data, loosely aligned with the central bank's expectations. In turn, real GDP growth is expected at 4.0% in the third quarter, indicating robust economic activity and adding leeway for higher interest rates to combat inflation.

### 2026-11-12 19:23:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Serbia Inflation Rate Rises Further in October — Serbia's annual inflation rate rose to 4.4% in October 2026 from 4.1% in September, reaching its highest level since August 2025 and accelerating for a third consecutive month. Price growth accelerated across most categories. Food prices rebounded, rising 1.2% after falling 1.3%, while inflation picked up for transportation (10.9% vs 10.1%), furnishings and household equipment (3% vs 2.9%), information and communication (2.3% vs 2.2%), clothing and footwear (3.1% vs 1.8%) and education (2.1% vs 1.2%). On the other hand, inflation eased for housing and utilities (5.2% vs 9.7%), restaurants and hotels (5.4% vs 5.6%), insurance and financial services (3% vs 3.5%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (6.4% vs 6.6%), health (7.4% vs 7.7%) and recreation and culture (8.7% vs 9.1%). From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.8%, up from 0.2% in September.

【全國支援小微企業融資協調工作機制培訓會在廣西南寧舉辦】11月10日，國家發展改革委財政金融和信用建設司、金融監管總局普惠金融司在廣西壯族自治區南寧市召開全國支援小微企業融資協調工作機制培訓會，總結交流工作機制成效，並就下一步推動小微企業金融服務實現穩投放、優結構、提質量、可持續作出安排部署。會議指出，小微企業是國民經濟和社會發展的重要組成部分，做好小微企業金融服務是踐行金融工作政治性、人民性的重要體現。工作機制執行兩年來，各地專班廣泛走訪企業經營主體，推動完善跨部門“聯合會診”機制，持續加大對服務業等重點領域的投入力度，小微企業融資難、融資貴等問題得到緩解。會議要求，各地專班要樹立和踐行正確政績觀，持續發揮好支援小微企業融資協調工作機制作用，積極拓展融資對接渠道，著力深化信用資訊共享機制，不斷提升小微企業金融服務的獲得感和滿意度。

### 2026-11-12 19:09:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Currency #Importance 1

Swiss Franc Stays Weak on Diverging Rate Outlooks — The Swiss franc weakened past 0.83 per USD, close to its lowest level since May of 2025, amid rising expectations of another Fed rate hike, which would further widen the interest rate differential between the US and Switzerland. Stronger-than-expected inflation and labour report in the US lifted expectations of further Fed hikes and boosted the greenback. By contrast, Swiss borrowing costs are expected to remain at 0%, the world's lowest, supporting the franc's appeal as a funding currency for carry trades, where investors pursue higher-yielding assets abroad. While the SNB has dialled down its threat of currency interventions, officials reiterated to lawmakers on Wednesday they stand ready to intervene if necessary to counter sharp appreciation. Nevertheless, the franc's renewed safe-haven status amid worries over a eurozone sovereign debt crisis and risks regarding AI erodes carry trade returns and is prompting an unwinding of these positions, adding upward pressure on the currency.

### 2026-11-12 18:55:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Pauses Rally — India’s BSE Sensex closed almost flat at 81,911 on Thursday, paring early gains amid volatile trading but remaining near its highest level since February. Investors booked profits following a nine-session rally, which had been supported by sustained foreign fund inflows, softer oil prices and solid corporate earnings. Meanwhile, attention shifted to the release of India's October retail inflation data for fresh cues on the interest-rate outlook. Sectoral performance was mixed, with strong buying in heavyweight financials offseting losses in autos, tech, FMGC and metal stocks. Non-bank lender Bajaj Finance rose 3.3% and was the standout performer, after announcing a share sale of over $1billion to institutional investors. Bajaj Finserv added 1.3%. ### 2026-11-12 18:47:51  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

India Inflation Breaches RBI Threshold — The headline inflation rate in India rose to 6.09% in October 2026 from 5.54% in the previous month, firmly above market expectations of 5.93%. With that, the rate crossed the Reserve Bank of India's upper target threshold of 6% for the first time since October 2024. Food prices, which make up a large portion of the Indian consumer basket, rose by 8.94% annually, picking up from the 7.74% increase in the previous month. Inflation was also elevated for restaurants and accommodation (10.06%) and personal care, social protection, and miscellaneous goods (10.14%). Meanwhile, transportation inflation was at 5.84%, extending its reversal from lower levels earlier in the year as the surge in oil prices—a commodity that India imports in large quantities—lifted fuel costs for consumers. From the previous month, Indian consumer prices rose by 0.51%.

He said service drops when a terminal moves faster than 161 km/h (100 mph), impairing efforts to intercept Russian jet-powered drones. ### 2026-11-12 18:09:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Montenegro #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Montenegro Inflation Rate Climbs to 3-Year High — Montenegro’s annual inflation rate rose to 5.9% in October 2026, its highest level since October 2023, up from 5.2% in September. The acceleration was driven by faster price growth in transport to 20.3% from 18.5% in the prior month, housing and utilities (1.9% vs 1.5%) and food (3.5% vs 2.6%), alongside furnishings (3.7% vs 2%), recreation, sports and culture (4.1% vs 3.5%), and education (0.3% vs 0.1%). Inflation stood at 4.4% for health. By contrast, price growth eased in clothing and footwear (3.5% vs 4.1%) and restaurants and hotels (5.8% vs 6.6%). From the previous month, consumer prices rose by 0.5% in October, unchanged from September. Meanwhile, the annual harmonized inflation rate rose to 5.9% from 5.5%, reaching its highest level since March 2024.

### 2026-11-12 17:48:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Mining Production #Importance 1

South Africa Mining Output Falls Sharply Again — Mining production in South Africa plunged by 10.7% year-on-year in September 2026, following a revised 11.7% slump in August, the steepest annual decline since October 2022. This marked the fifth consecutive month of downturn and at a solid pace, amid broad-based declines across categories. The main drags on mining activity were iron ore (-23.7%), coal (-13.4%), PGMs (-9.9%) and diamonds (-63.1%). Output also declined significantly for gold (-7.5%), building materials (-12.5%), other metallic minerals (-11.6%), other non-metallic minerals (-9.5%) and nickel (-7.7%). Meanwhile, production increased for chromium ore (+13.1%) and manganese ore (+12.8%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, mining production rose by 2.8%, rebounding from a revised 4.8% decline previously. Seasonally adjusted mining production dropped by 6% in the third quarter of 2026 compared with the second quarter of 2026.

### 2026-11-12 17:40:28  #國際

【俄副外長透露部分俄美烏三方會談細節】俄羅斯副外長里亞布科夫當地時間11月12日在接受採訪時透露了部分此前在阿聯酋首都阿布扎比舉行的俄美烏三方會談中的部分談判細節，其中涉及歐洲安全體系的構建等內容。01，構建新的歐洲安全體系，里亞布科夫表示，在剛剛結束的阿布扎比俄美烏三方會談中，各方關注如何構建旨在避免局勢升級並轉向共存的歐洲安全體系。他指出，目前還不能談及相關方之間信任的恢復。但結合會談中討論的內容，可以在具備必要政治意願的情況下，推動建設某種行之有效的安全體系，以避免不可控的局勢升級。02，“歐洲國家參與或致談判進展倒退”，里亞布科夫還表示，歐洲國家參與烏克蘭問題談判，可能導致此前取得的進展出現倒退，甚至使現有談判努力遭到破壞。里亞布科夫稱，俄羅斯總統普京將向美國談判代表闡明俄方立場，隨後美國的任務就是讓其歐洲盟友認清形勢、恢復理性。他表示，目前歐洲國家尚未表現出理性解決問題的意願，仍傾向於繼續支援烏克蘭。03，俄方認可美有意消除分歧的態度，此外，里亞布科夫說，在阿布扎比會談期間討論的部分方案遭到俄方反對。他強調，美國方面有意消除各方分歧，俄方希望這一態度能夠有助於解決問題。 (央視新聞)

UK Q3 Construction Orders Fall at Steeper Pace — UK construction orders declined 25.3% year-on-year in Q3 2026, deepening from a revised 18.5% decline in the previous quarter and marking a third consecutive quarter of contraction. This was the steepest annual drop since Q4 2023, driven by a sharper fall in orders for other work (-27.5% vs -19.0% in Q2), particularly private commercial orders (-47.8% vs -28.9%). Private industrial orders also declined (-22.0%), although at a slower pace than in Q2 (-60.4%). Meanwhile, other infrastructure orders edged up 0.4%, reversing a 22.0% decline, while other new work excluding infrastructure rebounded (44.5% vs -46.1%). New housing orders fell 16.6%, reversing an 8.9% increase in Q2, as both private housing (-22.6% vs 14.5%) and public housing orders (-13.5% vs -18.1%) declined. On a quarterly basis, construction orders rose 1.2%, recovering from an 11.9% decline in Q2 and marking the first increase in four quarters.

### 2026-11-12 15:33:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Manufacturing Production MoM #Importance 1

UK Manufacturing Output Beats Expectations in September — Manufacturing production in the UK grew by 0.7% month-on-month in September 2026, beating market expectations for a 0.5% decline and matching the pace recorded in the previous month. This remained the fastest growth since March, as output increased in seven of the thirteen manufacturing subsectors, with the largest positive contribution coming from the manufacture of food products, beverages and tobacco (2.9% vs -0.5% in August). Moreover, production rebounded sharply in coke and refined petroleum products (12.2% vs -7.4%) and electrical equipment (3.8% vs -2.2%), while growth in chemicals and chemical products accelerated (1.3% vs 0.8%). In contrast, the largest negative contribution came from basic metals and metal products (-2.1% vs 0.3%). On an annual basis, manufacturing production increased by 4.4% in September, the fastest rise since March 2024, exceeding forecasts of 3.8% and accelerating from a 2.5% gain in the preceding period.

This marked a slower pace of growth from 1.8% in the previous period, as corporate spending moderated. The slowdown was largely driven by weaker investment in dwellings and other machinery and equipment, while spending on transport equipment, ICT equipment, and intellectual property products increased significantly. Romania Inflation at 3-Month High — Romania's annual inflation rate accelerated to 6.9% in October 2026, up from 6.6% in September, marking its highest level in three months. The acceleration was driven primarily by faster price growth in non-food goods (7.7% vs 7.1% in September), while inflation also edged up for food goods (3.1% vs 3.0%) and services (11.2% vs 11.1%). Among non-food items, fuel prices remained a major contributor, with petrol (32.9% vs 28.4%) and diesel prices (43.5% vs 37.6%) surging. Significant price increases were also recorded for rent (38.5%) and water supply and related services (13.7%). Within food goods, milk and dairy products saw the sharpest price growth among major categories (6.8% vs. 4.6%), followed by bread and bakery products (4.4% vs. 4.8%) and meat and meat products (3.5% vs. 4.1%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.82% in October, accelerating from 0.77% in September. Meanwhile, the HICP-based annual inflation rate rose to 7.0% from 6.5% in previous month.

UK Trade Surplus Largest in 5 Months — The United Kingdom’s trade deficit widened to £5.13 billion in September 2026, up from a revised £2.57 billion in the previous month, marking the largest trade gap since April. Exports grew 2.7% month-on-month to a record high of £83.38 billion, while imports increased 5.7% to a record peak of £88.52 billion. Goods exports rose 5.2% to £36.76 billion, boosted by increases in shipments to both EU and non-EU countries. Shipments to EU countries rose 4.9%, due to higher exports of chemicals, machinery and transport equipment, while those to non-EU countries grew 7.8%, mainly supported by rises in machinery and transport equipment. Services exports rose 0.8% to £46.63 billion. On the import side, goods imports climbed 8.2% to £59.05 billion, with imports from non-EU countries rising 4.5%, mainly due to increases in chemicals. Meanwhile, imports from EU countries rose 9.5%, mainly driven by increases in machinery and transport equipment. Services imports rose 0.9% to £29.47 billion.

### 2026-11-12 15:13:01  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sweden #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Sweden Inflation Rate Confirmed at 0.9% in October — Consumer prices in Sweden rose by 0.9% year-on-year in October 2026, confirming initial estimates and slowing from a 1.1% gain in September, which was the fastest increase since February 2025. The slowdown was mainly driven by the ongoing deflation in food and non-alcoholic beverages (-7% vs -6.6% in September), furnishings, household equipment and maintenance (-1.6% vs -0.5%), health (-6.3% vs -5.9%), and education services (at -15.6%). Conversely, housing and utilities (5.1% vs 4.8%) and transport prices (4.3% vs 3.4%) accelerated, driven by a 25% surge in fuel costs, including increases of 38% in diesel and 16% in gasoline. Electricity prices also climbed 14%, while rents rose 3.3%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged, after rising 0.9% in September. Meanwhile, the CPIF, which excludes the effect of changes in mortgage interest rates, rose by 1.1% year-on-year, slowing from a four-month high of 1.5% gain in the previous month, while its monthly reading remained flat.

### 2026-11-12 15:07:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Monthly GDP MoM #Importance 1

UK Economy Posts Surprise Growth in September — The UK economy expanded 0.2% month-on-month in September 2026, compared with forecasts for no change and following a revised flat reading in August. Services output rose 0.2% after falling 0.1% in the previous month, mainly driven by growth in real estate activities (0.4%) and transportation and storage (1.4%). Construction output also increased 0.8%, ending four consecutive monthly declines, with both new work and repair and maintenance activity recording gains of 0.9% and 0.6%, respectively. Meanwhile, production output grew 0.3%, slowing from a 0.7% increase in August, as gains in manufacturing (0.7%) and mining and quarrying (1.4%) were partially offset by declines in water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation activities (-1.7%), and electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply (-0.3%). On an annual basis, GDP grew 2.1% in September, beating expectations for a 1.9% increase and marking the strongest growth since October 2022.

### 2026-11-12 15:06:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 2

UK GDP Growth Strongest Since 2024 — The UK economy expanded by 0.7% quarter-on-quarter in Q3 2026, marking its fastest growth since Q1 2024. Growth accelerated from 0.5% in Q2 and was in line with market expectations, preliminary estimates showed. Services output rose 0.9% (vs. 0.6% in Q2), led by administrative and support service activities, which increased 4.1%, and information and communication, which grew 2.1%. Production output edged up 0.1% (vs. -0.1%), as a 0.9% increase in manufacturing offset declines in mining and quarrying (-2.2%), electricity and gas (-2.1%), and water supply (-1.5%). Meanwhile, construction output fell 0.7%, as a 1.6% drop in new work outweighed a 0.5% increase in repair and maintenance. On the expenditure side, gross fixed capital formation rose 1.7%, while household and government consumption both increased 0.6%. Exports grew 1.6%, while imports rose 1.8%. On an annual basis, GDP expanded by 1.9%, its strongest growth in nearly two years, accelerating from 1.4% in Q2.

### 2026-11-12 14:14:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Current Account #Importance 1

Finland Current Account Balance Swings to Deficit — Finland posted a current account deficit of EUR 541 million in September 2026, compared with a surplus of EUR 34 million in the same month a year earlier. The goods account shifted to a deficit of EUR 283 million from a surplus of EUR 286 million in the previous year, as imports rose 22%, outpacing the 13% increase in exports. The secondary income deficit also widened to EUR 157 million from EUR 99 million. Meanwhile, the services deficit narrowed to EUR 595 million from EUR 613 million, while the primary income surplus increased to EUR 493 million from EUR 460 million. The 12-month moving total of the current account was EUR 1.7 billion in deficit. Separately, net capital inflow to Finland from abroad amounted to EUR 2.5 billion in September, with other investments accounting for the largest inflow at EUR 1.6 billion. Meanwhile, direct investments recorded the largest net capital outflow at EUR 0.3 billion.

Meanwhile, Bank Indonesia continued intervening in currency markets to support the rupiah ahead of next week’s policy meeting. ### 2026-11-12 11:55:39  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Dollar Hits 9-Month Low — The Australian dollar fell below $0.69, hitting its lowest level since late January as the US dollar strengthened on growing expectations of a Federal Reserve interest rate hike. The odds of a half-percentage-point Fed rate hike in December surged after US inflation accelerated more than expected in October and policymakers signaled willingness to tighten policy more aggressively. In contrast, weak housing data and a significant drop in both consumer and business confidence in Australia prompted traders to largely rule out a further interest rate increase by the Reserve Bank of Australia in December. After the central bank had increased interest rates five times this year, which brought the cash rate to an eighteen-year high of 4.85%, markets now imply an 82% probability that rates have peaked, with a quarter-point rate cut fully priced in by the end of 2027. Meanwhile, a broader selloff in global equity markets added pressure to the risk-sensitive Aussie.

### 2026-11-12 11:48:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10-Year Yield Holds Advance — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield hovered around 2.78% on Thursday, holding onto its gains following a sharp increase in the previous session amid expectations of tighter monetary policy. Recent data showed that Japanese producer prices climbed 7.1% year-on-year in October, surpassing forecasts of 6.5% and accelerating from September’s 6.8% increase. The stronger figures have reinforced expectations of further Bank of Japan rate hikes, with policymakers anticipated to raise borrowing costs in December and continue tightening into next year. The BOJ’s latest Summary of Opinions also indicated broad support among policymakers for additional rate increases, as underlying inflation has reached levels broadly consistent with its 2% price stability target. However, board member Toichiro Asada urged caution over the timing of further hikes, noting that household consumption remains weak and recent price increases have not necessarily been fueled by strong demand.

【香港修訂法例防止犯國安罪行人士在校任教】香港特區政府將於明日（13日）在憲報刊登《2026年教育（修訂）條例草案》，以引入教師執業證明書要求，完善教師序號產生器制，並設立機制處理被裁定干犯危害國家安全罪行人士的教師註冊事宜，以確保所有教師均為適合及適當人選，維護專業水平。 據瞭解，《條例草案》將訂明法定要求，規定所有公帑資助學校、私立學校、國際學校及幼稚園的教師（包括校長），必須持有教師註冊及執業證明書，方可在學校任教，以確保教師的水平。執業證明書的有效期將不超過三個學年。《條例草案》亦會列明申請執業證明書所需的條件，當中包括教師必須符合持續專業發展要求，方可取得執業證明書，以確保所有教師緊貼全球和本地教育政策的最新發展，使其知識和教學法能夠切合學生及社會需要，從而進一步提升教師團隊的專業質素。 《條例草案》亦會設立防護機制，防止被裁定干犯國安罪行的人士在學校任教，以有效保護學生，維護教師的專業操守，維持公眾對教育專業的信心。

【日本報告本季首起禽流感疫情 】日本農林水產省12日說，京都府一個家禽飼養場出現高致病性禽流感。這是日本確認的本季第一起禽流感疫情。每年秋冬到次年春季是日本的禽流感流行季。日本農林水產省12日釋出的新聞公報說，發現禽流感的家禽飼養場位於京都府龜岡市，共飼養了約20萬隻蛋雞。12日上午，基因檢測結果確認其飼養的家禽感染了高致病性禽流感病毒。公報說，根據相關防疫政策，有關部門將撲殺處理該飼養場的全部家禽，將周邊半徑3公里以內範圍劃為“移動限制區域”，將周邊半徑3至10公里範圍劃為“搬出限制區域”。

### 2026-11-12 11:22:43  #國際

【劉海星：共同夯實中美建設性戰略穩定關係基礎】11月12日，中共中央對外聯絡部部長劉海星在北京出席2026中美政黨對話。劉海星在主旨講話中表示，習近平總書記指出，未來50年，國際關係中最重要的事情是中美必須找到正確的相處之道。面向未來，中美應秉持相互尊重、和平共處、合作共贏的原則，一道探索新時代兩個大國正確相處之道，共同努力夯實中美建設性戰略穩定關係的基礎。一是樹立正確認知，加深對彼此戰略走向、發展道路的瞭解，共同加強中美建設性戰略穩定關係的思想基礎。二是有效管控分歧，堅持相互尊重、求同存異，努力把矛盾點轉化為合作點，共同增進中美建設性戰略穩定關係的信任基礎。三是拓展互利合作，拉長合作清單，壓縮問題清單，共同夯實中美建設性戰略穩定關係的利益基礎。四是促進人文交流，搭建更多交往對話的橋樑，共同厚植中美建設性戰略穩定關係的民意基礎。五是承擔大國責任，加強在重大國際和地區問題上的溝通協調，共同擴大中美建設性戰略穩定關係的國際基礎。劉海星在主旨講話中表示，放眼亞太，在地區各國的共同努力下，亞太地區總體保持和平與發展態勢，但同時也面臨地緣政治、單邊主義和保護主義上升等挑戰。中美同為亞太地區國家、世界前兩大經濟體，中美關係是世界上最重要的雙邊關係之一，在當前形勢下，雙方共同利益不是減少了，而是更多了。正如習近平主席指出，“雖然中美合作不可能解決世界上所有問題，但沒有兩國合作，世界上很多問題都難以解決”。一個穩定、健康、可持續發展的中美關係不僅符合兩國人民利益，也是亞太和世界和平繁榮的重要保障。（中聯部）

### 2026-11-12 11:01:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Holds Firm on Hawkish Fed Bets — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note hovered near 5% during Thursday’s post-holiday session, holding onto gains from earlier in the week amid growing expectations of additional Federal Reserve rate hikes. The rise was fueled by stronger-than-expected October inflation data and increasingly hawkish signals from Fed policymakers. Fed Governor Christopher Waller indicated that a 50-basis-point increase in December could be justified, while Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan supported at least two more 25-basis-point hikes as inflationary pressures persist. Markets are currently pricing in approximately a 91% probability of a rate increase next month, with further tightening in January and potentially April also being considered. Investors are now awaiting Thursday’s initial jobless claims and existing home sales reports for further indications of inflationary trends, labor market conditions and the broader economic outlook.

【Yorkville Ives：若黃仁勳卸任CEO 將把英偉達目標價下調約25%】前韋德布什證券公司分析師丹·艾夫斯創辦的Yorkville Ives表示，如果沒有黃仁勳，英偉達的目標價將從目前的每股300美元下調約25%至225美元。艾夫斯等分析師在報告中寫道，現實是，在公眾、客戶和科技業眼中，過去那個黃仁勳如今已經不復存在；隨著更多訊息曝光，董事會也迅速意識到，情勢正一天比一天惡化。他們補充稱，接下來的關鍵問題包括：誰將接替黃仁勳、過渡期間的策略路線圖為何、董事會是否會有人事變動、該公司將如何應對政府調查等。不過，分析師們表示，該公司及其領先業界的GPU技術仍將繼續向前發展，這不會改變英偉達在AI革命晶片軍備競賽中已根深蒂固的地位。

### 2026-11-12 10:48:31  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Indonesian Shares Rally as Political Jitters Ease — Indonesian stocks jumped 80 points, or 1.6%, to 5,191 in Thursday morning deals, recovering from the prior session's modest fall as all sectors advanced. Sentiment improved as political uncertainty moderated after Vice President Gibran Rakabuming submitted his resignation following a Supreme Court ruling concerning the validity of his high-school diploma. Traders also welcomed assurances of 4.5 million tons of rice supply ahead of year-end, easing concerns over food prices amid severe El Niño conditions. Regional sentiment gained support from China's pledge to strengthen monetary policy measures and boost domestic demand. Darma Henwa (8.5%), Bumi Resources (5.8%), Bukit Asam (3.7%), and Indosat (2.2%) led early gains. However, caution ahead of Bank Indonesia's policy meeting next week and concerns over the rupiah-growth trade-off capped optimism. Meanwhile, MSCI would remove Barito Pacific from its indexes, adding to worries over Indonesia's efforts to retain emerging-market status.

【湖南證監局：對湘財證券採取責令改正、暫停新開證券賬戶3個月等措施】湖南證監局釋出行政監管措施決定書。經查，湘財證券存在以下問題：一是與經紀業務相關的內部控制執行不到位；二是客戶異常交易行為管理落實不到位；三是資訊科技合規與風險管理不到位；四是對員工廉潔從業管理不到位。湖南證監局決定對湘財證券採取如下監督管理措施，並記入證券期貨市場誠信檔案：一是責令公司對上述問題限期改正，並在收到本決定書後3個月內向我局提交書面整改報告。二是責令公司自收到本決定書之日起暫停新開證券賬戶3個月，暫停期間公司不得新增經紀業務客戶。三是責令公司根據有關制度規定，對涉及的責任人員進行處分，並自作出處分決定之日起5個工作日內向我局書面報告。

【李彥宏談AI變革時代百度踩過的坑】11月12日，在百度世界大會上，百度董事長李彥宏表示行業的下一幕是智慧組織，並談及在AI變革時代百度曾經踩過的一些坑。 他表示，早期大模型剛出現時，百度工程師們熱衷用開源模型做SFT（監督微調），業務應用效果一度不錯，但模型升級後原有成果很快落後。為此百度要求業務部門不能自行訓練模型，統一由專門模型部門負責，避免“熵增”、提升效率。後來智慧體等新熱點出現，業務部門又紛紛跟進，導致資源分散、成功率反而降低。百度隨後透過每月開會要求聚焦，把努力方向“從多變少”，讓大家做更少的事，以提升效率和成功率。

Hong Kong Stocks Fall for Second Day — The Hang Seng Index fell 1.2%, or 287 points, to around 22,031 on Thursday, extending losses for a second consecutive session as weakness across technology, retail and financial stocks weighed on the market. Technology sentiment was pressured by Nvidia’s 8% decline in after-hours US trading following a Wall Street Journal report that the chipmaker was preparing to oust CEO Jensen Huang, raising concerns over leadership uncertainty and adding volatility to the global AI sector. Moreover, Hong Kong’s IPO market showed signs of losing steam, with all six companies listing this week pricing shares near the bottom of their marketed ranges amid weak institutional demand. Investors also remained cautious over US monetary policy following stronger inflation data and increased expectations of a December Federal Reserve rate hike. Among notable laggards were Tencent (-1.6%), AIA (-0.7%), Meituan (-1.9%), Alibaba (-2.6%), and CK Hutchison (-3.3%).

PBoC Vows Stronger Support for Domestic Demand and Growth — The People's Bank of China (PBoC) pledged to strengthen counter-cyclical adjustments, boost domestic demand, and improve supply to support sustained, higher-quality economic growth, according to its Q3 monetary policy report. The central bank said it would maintain a flexible, targeted and forward-looking policy approach, keeping liquidity ample and financing conditions accommodative while aligning money supply and credit growth with economic expansion and price-level objectives. It also plans to expand structural policy support for domestic demand, technological innovation, small businesses and key infrastructure, while diversifying loan interest rate pricing benchmarks beyond the loan prime rate (LPR). On the yuan, the board reiterated its commitment to a managed floating exchange rate, aiming to stabilise market expectations, guard against excessive currency swings and keep the yuan broadly stable at a reasonable and balanced level.

### 2026-11-12 09:58:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

BoJ Asada Urges Caution on Further Rate Hikes Amid Weak Demand — Bank of Japan board member Toichiro Asada said further interest rate hikes should be carefully timed, warning that monetary tightening is having a growing impact on economic activity as policy rates move closer to neutral. He noted in a speech that private consumption remains subdued and recent price increases have not necessarily been driven by strong demand, raising the risk that additional tightening could weaken economic activity and push down underlying inflation. Although real wages have remained positive since early 2026, supported by three consecutive years of solid base-pay increases and higher bonuses, Asada stressed the need to assess actual consumer price developments before determining whether underlying inflation can sustainably exceed the 2% target. He added that further tightening would be more justified once wage growth translates into stronger demand-driven inflation.

### 2026-11-12 09:49:23  #公司

【知名科技分析師郭明錤評黃仁勳事件】郭明錤在社交平臺發文：黃仁勳對英偉達很重要，因為他能將工程師們認為可行的技術轉化為整個公司投入資源、承擔風險並必須交付的產品。這就是為什麼供應鏈能獲得如此強大的訂單可見度，並願意跟上激進的規格升級步伐。這也迫使競爭對手跟進，從而推動整個AI行業的技術升級和供應鏈投資。兩者之間，與賦予供應鏈訂單可見度相比，決定激進的規格升級並使其實現是更難的部分。黃仁勳不必是英偉達最瞭解HVDC、CPO/NPO或CCL/PTFE等新技術的人。他的價值在於，他能同時權衡戰略、組織、技術和供應鏈，然後快速做出決定並投入資源支援。根據目前公開的資訊來看，黃仁勳很難毫髮無損地度過這一關。如果是這樣，務實的做法可能是逐步減少英偉達對黃仁勳個人決策的依賴，同時準備繼任計劃。在此過程中，有兩件事值得關注，以判斷AI技術和投資的走向：英偉達是否能像以往一樣高效地繼續開發新產品和技術，以及競爭對手如何回應。供應鏈研究將是這一判斷的重要依據。

### 2026-11-12 09:46:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Holds Losses on Hawkish Fed View — Silver traded below $52 an ounce on Thursday, holding recent losses as a stronger US dollar weighed on the precious metal. The dollar gained support from hotter-than-expected US inflation figures and increasingly hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials, reinforcing expectations of further interest rate increases. Fed Governor Christopher Waller indicated that a 50-basis-point hike in December may be necessary, while Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan supported at least two additional 25-basis-point increases to address persistent inflationary pressures. Markets are currently pricing in approximately a 91% probability of a rate hike next month, with further increases in January and potentially April also being factored in. Investors are now turning their attention to upcoming US economic releases, including weekly jobless claims, existing home sales and producer inflation data, for further indications of the Fed’s monetary policy outlook.

【中國海油：在渤海中部海域取得超深層油氣勘探重大突破】中國海洋石油有限公司在渤海超5000米的超深層地層鑽探發現一口火山岩高產油氣井，標誌著中國在海上超深層天然氣勘探領域取得重大突破。該井位於渤海中部海域，緊鄰渤海最大的富烴凹陷—渤中凹陷，在火山岩地層中累計鑽遇超120米天然氣層，完鑽井深5770米。經測試，該井日產天然氣約80萬立方米（約2800萬立方英尺），展現了海上超深層火山岩領域廣闊的勘探前景，有力推動了渤中凹陷天然氣勘探程序。中國海洋石油集團首席科學家、總地質師徐長貴表示，近年來，中國海油持續加大高熱流強活動盆地深層—超深層油氣富集理論創新與關鍵技術攻關力度，中國海域勘探實現了從淺層到深層—超深層的重大跨越。該井的成功鑽探開闢了海上超深層天然氣勘探新區帶，拓展了渤海海域深層油氣勘探空間，對加快渤海超深層勘探程序、建設渤海萬億方大氣區具有積極意義。

【“廣西梧州—平陸運河—越南胡志明”外貿航線開通】近日，滿載賀州刨花板的“華航900”輪從廣西北部灣國際港務集團有限公司（簡稱“北部灣港集團”）旗下北港西江港口梧州赤水港揚帆啟航。這是梧州首票經平陸運河報關發運的外貿貨物，也宣告“廣西梧州—平陸運河—越南胡志明”江海聯運外貿航線正式開通。該航線依託平陸運河通航契機，打通桂東、湘南腹地對接東盟的江海出海通道，是北部灣港集團服務西部陸海新通道、深化兩灣聯動發展的重要實踐。新航線相比傳統繞行珠三角出海路線，優勢突出。經測算，新航線單櫃綜合物流成本下降約10％，中轉環節大幅精簡，貨物週轉效率顯著提升。航線的開通，改變了梧州外貿長期單一向東出海的格局。依託平陸運河通江達海的獨特優勢，將西江腹地與東盟市場緊密相連，有效增強了腹地板材、製造等特色產品的國際市場競爭力，助力區域外貿穩規模、優結構。

### 2026-11-12 09:36:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 2

Yen Weakens to Over Two-Month Lows — The Japanese yen traded near the psychologically important 160-per-dollar threshold on Thursday after sliding for two straight sessions and falling to its weakest levels in more than two months, opening the door for possible currency intervention. The decline was driven by broad dollar strength amid rising expectations of a faster pace of Federal Reserve rate hikes, following hot US inflation data and increasingly hawkish comments from Fed officials. Markets are currently pricing in around a 91% chance of a Fed rate increase next month, with further hikes in January and April also being considered. Meanwhile, domestic data showed that Japan’s producer prices increased 7.1% year-on-year in October, exceeding market expectations of 6.5% and accelerating from September’s 6.8% gain. Those figures reinforce expectations of tighter monetary policy from the Bank of Japan, which is also widely expected to raise interest rates next month and continue its tightening cycle into next year.

However, Nvidia fell sharply in after-hours trading following reports of potential leadership changes and allegations involving Huang, weighing on sentiment toward technology shares. ### 2026-11-12 09:21:28  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Currency #Importance 1

Dollar Holds Gains on Hawkish Fed Bets — The US dollar index hovered just below 103 on Thursday after gaining more than 1% over the previous two sessions, reaching its highest level since April 2025 amid growing expectations of further Federal Reserve rate hikes. The rally was supported by October inflation data that came in above market forecasts, alongside increasingly hawkish remarks from Fed policymakers. Fed Governor Christopher Waller cautioned that a 50-basis-point rate increase in December may be necessary, while Dallas Fed President Lorie Logan supported at least two additional 25-basis-point hikes as inflationary pressures continue to build. Markets are now assigning roughly a 91% probability to a rate increase next month, with investors also factoring in the possibility of further tightening in January and potentially April. Attention now shifts to Thursday’s initial jobless claims and existing home sales figures for additional insight into inflation trends, labor market conditions and the broader economic outlook.

### 2026-11-12 09:13:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

New Zealand Dollar Holds Losses — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.555 on Thursday, after falling for the previous two sessions as the US dollar strengthened on increased bets for a Federal Reserve rate hike in December. Hotter-than-expected US inflation in October, coupled with hawkish remarks from several Fed officials, fueled expectations of tighter US monetary policy. Meanwhile, preliminary election results in New Zealand indicating the incumbent National-led coalition could retain power offered support for the kiwi, as this would likely ensure continuity in the Reserve Bank’s sole focus on inflation and the government’s spending policies. Conversely, the main opposition Labour Party’s plan to reinstate the central bank’s dual mandate of price stability and employment was viewed as a potential drag on the currency, as it could tilt monetary policy in a dovish direction given persistent weakness in the labor market. However, the outcome remains uncertain, with official results due on November 27.

【青花郎、國窖1573零售均價重跌 飛天茅臺、精品茅臺連續下行 洋河夢之藍M6+、五糧液普五八代逆勢上漲】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，11月12日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價四漲五跌三平，下跌品種的數量稍微超出，但累計跌幅明顯超過上漲端，整體價盤再度大跌，又一次創下近兩個月以來新低。上漲端，五糧液普五八代上漲3元至783元，連續第二天穩健攀升，已走出近期最低谷；五糧液1618上漲1元至817元，小幅上漲結束兩連陰，但依然維持在近30天最低點附近；洋河夢之藍M6+上漲5元至585元，昨日大跌後強勁反彈，成為今天漲幅最高的單品；習酒君品上漲2元至612元，也擺脫兩連跌頹勢，在過去一個月以來的最低紀錄附近整理。下跌端，飛天茅臺的零售均價下跌4元至1766元，連續第三天下跌，近期其價格呈緩步下行態勢；精品茅臺下跌5元至2420元，亦為三連陰，再次創下近30天新低，昨天起包括精品茅臺在內的16款貴州茅臺酒產品在i茅臺上採用全天動態補貨的模式投放，其售價為2359元/瓶與此前定價保持一致；國窖1573下跌6元至852元，連續九天來非跌即平，持續重新整理過去一個月來最低紀錄；青花郎重跌10元至653元，三連陰且跌幅日增，再度創下30天以來新低，是今日跌幅最重的單品。水晶劍南春下跌1元至400元，再度回退至400元關口，近期其價格圍繞這一整百點位多次振盪。持平方面，茅臺1935的零售均價連續第二天平盤，維持在685元，保持高位休整狀態；青花汾20仍為378元，維持在過去一個月最低點附近；古井貢古20保持在510元，昨日重跌後陣腳暫穩。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價再度大幅下挫，青花郎重跌10元，國窖1573下跌6元，精品茅臺下跌5元，是導致整體價盤下行的主要力量。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10464元，較昨日大幅下跌15元，連續第三天創下今年9月中旬以來的階段性新低。
